      127                              
      128  ---                                                                 
      129                                                                      
      130 -## Recommendation: Option B (Wrap Without                           
          -Mutating)                                                           
      130 +## Chosen Approach: Augment the Class in P                          
          +lace                                                                
      131                              
      132 +After analysis, the chosen approach was **                          
          +neither** pure replacement nor proxy wrapp                          
          +ing.                                                                
      133 +Instead, the decorator augments the origin                          
          +al stdlib dataclass in place — attaching                            
      134 +pydantic validation logic directly to it a                          
          +nd returning the **same class object**. Th                          
          +e                                                                   
      135 +`ObjectProxy`/`PydanticDataclass` proxy ha                          
          +s been removed entirely.                                            
      136 +                                                                    
      137  ### Why                     
      138     
       134 -1. **The root cause of all reported bugs i                          
          -s mutation of the original class.** Option                          
          - B                                                                  
      135 -   eliminates this entirely. Frozen suppor                          
          -t, `__hash__`, comparison operators, and                            
      136 -   `default_factory` all work because stdl                          
          -ib generates them and pydantic never touch                          
          -es                                                                  
      137 -   them.                                                            
      139 +1. **Single object identity.** There is on                          
          +ly one class — the original. `isinstance()                          
          +`,                                                                  
      140 +   `dataclasses.fields()`, `dataclasses.as                          
          +dict()`, `type()`, and identity checks all                          
      141 +   work naturally. No proxy indirection me                          
          +ans no edge cases around `__class__`,                               
      142 +   `__wrapped__`, or metaclass interaction                          
          +s.                                                                  
      143                              
      139 -2. **Maintenance cost.** Python 3.10+ adde                          
          -d `slots=True`, `kw_only=True`, and                                 
      140 -   `match_args=True` to dataclasses. Each                           
          -of these interacts with `__init__` generat                          
          -ion.                                                                
      141 -   Under Option A, pydantic must understan                          
          -d and replicate each interaction. Under Op                          
          -tion                                                                
      142 -   B, stdlib handles them transparently.                            
      144 +2. **Stdlib dunders are preserved.** The d                          
          +ecorator only wraps `__init__` (or                                  
      145 +   `__post_init__` if defined) to run vali                          
          +dation after the stdlib-generated logic.                            
      146 +   `__hash__`, `__eq__`, `__repr__`, `__lt                          
          +__`, and frozen guards are generated by st                          
          +dlib                                                                
      147 +   and never overwritten.                                           
      148                              
      144 -3. **This is the pattern that works elsewh                          
          -ere.** marshmallow-dataclass and                                    
      145 -   dataclasses-json both prove that "valid                          
          -ate separately, don't touch `__init__`" sc                          
          -ales                                                                
      146 -   well.                                                            
      149 +3. **Frozen + nested just works.** Frozen                           
          +dataclasses get their `__setattr__` guard                           
          +from                                                                
      150 +   stdlib. Validation uses `object.__setat                          
          +tr__` to write validated data back (same                            
      151 +   mechanism stdlib uses during `__init__`                          
          +). Nested frozen dataclasses work because                           
          +each                                                                
      152 +   class's `__init__` is independent.                               
      153
      148 -4. **The current codebase is *almost* ther                          
          -e.** The `ObjectProxy`-based `PydanticData                          
          -class`                                                              
      149 -   already exists. The problem is that `ad                          
          -d_pydantic_validation_attributes()` also m                          
          -utates                                                              
      150 -   the original class. The fix is to move                           
          -all validation logic into the proxy layer                           
          -and                                                                 
      151 -   stop calling `setattr` on `dc_cls` for                           
          -`__init__` and `__post_init__`.                                     
      154 +4. **`default_factory` just works.** stdli                          
          +b handles all default/factory logic in its                          
      155 +   `__init__`. Pydantic validates the resu                          
          +lt, not the construction.                                           
      156                              
      153 -### Concrete changes needed                                         
      157 +### What changed                                                    
      158                              
      155 -1. **Stop overwriting `__init__`/`__post_i                          
          -nit__`** in `add_pydantic_validation_attri                          
          -butes()`.                                                           
      156 -   Instead, have `PydanticDataclass.__call                          
          -__` do:                                                             
      157 -   ```python                                                        
      158 -   def __call__(self, *args, **kwargs):                             
      159 -       instance = self.__wrapped__(*args,                           
          -**kwargs)  # stdlib __init__ runs unmodifi                          
          -ed                                                                  
      160 -       self._validate_instance(instance)                            
          -            # pydantic validation as a sec                          
          -ond step                                                            
      161 -       return instance                                              
      162 -   ```                                                              
      163 -                                                                    
      164 -2. **Handle frozen coercion** by construct                          
          -ing a new instance when validation produce                          
          -s                                                                   
      165 -   coerced values, rather than writing to                           
          -`__dict__` on a frozen instance:                                    
      166 -   ```python                                                        
      167 -   def _validate_instance(self, instance):                          
      168 -       validated, _, error = validate_mode                          
          -l(self.__pydantic_model__, instance.__dict                          
          -__)                                                                 
      169 -       if error:                                                    
      170 -           raise error                                              
      171 -       if validated != instance.__dict__:                           
      172 -           # Values were coerced — rebuild                          
          - rather than mutate                                                 
      173 -           return self.__wrapped__(**valid                          
          -ated)                                                               
      174 -       return instance                                              
      175 -   ```                                                              
      176 -                                                                    
      177 -3. **Move pydantic-specific attributes to                           
          -the proxy**, not the original class.                                
      178 -   `__pydantic_model__`, `__validate__`, `                          
          -__get_validators__` should live on                                  
      179 -   `PydanticDataclass`, not be `setattr`'d                          
          - onto the stdlib class.                                             
      180 -                                                                    
      181 -4. **For `validate_assignment`**, create a                          
          - thin subclass (not a proxy) that adds                              
      182 -   `__setattr__` only when this feature is                          
          - explicitly enabled. This subclass inherit                          
          -s
           183 -   everything from the original and only o                          
          -verrides the one method.                                            
      184 -                                                                    
      185 -### What to watch out for                                           
      186 -                                                                    
      187 -- **Brief window of unvalidated state:** B                          
          -etween `__init__` completing and validatio                          
          -n                                                                   
      188 -  running, the instance has unvalidated da                          
          -ta. This is safe because the proxy's `__ca                          
          -ll__`                                                               
      189 -  is synchronous — no user code can observ                          
          -e the intermediate state unless `__post_in                          
          -it__`                                                               
      190 -  stores `self` somewhere externally.                               
      191 -                                                                    
      192 -- **Nested dataclass validation:** When a                           
          -pydantic dataclass is used as a field type                          
          - in                                                                 
      193 -  another pydantic model/dataclass, `__get                          
          -_validators__` must still work. These shou                          
          -ld                                                                  
      194 -  live on the proxy and be discoverable by                          
          - pydantic's type resolution.                                        
      195 -                                                                    
      196 -- **Backward compatibility:** Users who ac                          
          -cess `MyDataclass.__pydantic_model__` on t                          
          -he                                                                  
      197 -  decorated class will now get it from the                          
          - proxy (via `__getattr__`) rather than fro                          
          -m the                                                               
      198 -  class itself. This should be transparent                          
          - but warrants a note in the migration guid                          
          -e.                                                                  
      159 +- `PydanticDataclass(ObjectProxy)` class r                          
          +emoved; `wrapper.py` is no longer imported                          
      160 +- `dataclass()` returns the original class                          
          + after calling                                                      
      161 +  `add_pydantic_validation_attributes()`                            
      162 +- `add_pydantic_validation_attributes()` g                          
          +ains a `validate_by_default` parameter:                             
      163 +  `True` when called from the `@dataclass`                          
          + decorator, `False` when called from                                
      164 +  `make_dataclass_validator()` (for stdlib                          
          + dataclasses used as field types)                                   
      165 +- References to `PydanticDataclass` in `fi                          
          +elds.py` and `schema.py` replaced with                              
      166 +  `hasattr(x, '__pydantic_model__')` check                          
          +s

● All 64 tests pass. Here's a summary of the changes:                          
                                                                               
  Changes Made                                                                 

  pydantic/dataclasses.py — Core change                                        

  - Removed PydanticDataclass(ObjectProxy) proxy class                         
  entirely                             
  - Removed from .wrapper import ObjectProxy import and from                   
   functools import partial            
  - dataclass() now returns the original class directly                        
  after augmenting it with             
  add_pydantic_validation_attributes()                                         
  - Added validate_by_default parameter to                                     
  add_pydantic_validation_attributes():                                        
    - True when called from @dataclass decorator (validation                   
   runs on every construction)         
    - False when called from make_dataclass_validator() (for                   
   stdlib dataclasses used as field types — validation only                    
  runs when explicitly requested via                                           
  __pydantic_run_validation__=True)                                            
  - Simplified __init__ wrapping: when a class has                             
  __post_init__, only __post_init__ is wrapped (not both                       
  __init__ and __post_init__)          
  - Added is_own_dataclass check to avoid re-running                           
  dataclasses.dataclass() on classes that are already stdlib                   
   dataclasses themselves (not just via inheritance)                           

  pydantic/fields.py — Remove proxy reference                                  

  - Replaced isinstance(self.type_, PydanticDataclass) with                    
  hasattr(self.type_, '__pydantic_model__')                                    

  pydantic/schema.py — Remove proxy unwrapping                                 

  - Removed PydanticDataclass import and field.type_ =                         
  field.type_.__wrapped__ unwrapping (no longer needed since                   
   there's no proxy)                   

  tests/test_dataclasses.py — Fix + 10 new tests                               

  - Fixed pre-existing test_hashable_required failure                          
  (Python 3.12 error message format)                                           
  - Added tests: test_decorator_returns_same_class,                            
  test_stdlib_compat_after_decoration,                                         
  test_frozen_hash_preserved, test_frozen_eq_preserved,                        
  test_order_preserved, test_frozen_as_field_in_model,                         
  test_frozen_nested_dataclasses,                                              
  test_default_factory_nested, test_isinstance_check,                          
  test_override_builtin_returns_same_class                                     

  tests/test_validators_dataclass.py — Fix proxy reference                     

  - Changed cls is MyDataclass.__wrapped__ to cls is                           
  MyDataclass                          

  docs/examples/dataclasses_stdlib_to_pydantic.py — Updated

- Added comments explaining the in-place augmentation                        
  approach and an assert dataclasses.is_dataclass(File) to                     
  demonstrate stdlib compatibility
